# Figma reference register

File key: `dv4ySB3t2ixsT8WkMXmiLA`. This is the exact source inventory captured during planning: **836 source nodes, 834 prototype frames, 48 foundation entries**. Node IDs, not names, identify references. Every source and prototype is retained in [figma-inventory.json](figma-inventory.json), including the prototype destination graph.

## Authority

[Completion contract](https://www.figma.com/design/dv4ySB3t2ixsT8WkMXmiLA/Daylight?node-id=70-72) defines responsive behavior and shared-workspace semantics. [Interaction guide](https://www.figma.com/design/dv4ySB3t2ixsT8WkMXmiLA/Daylight?node-id=39-317) defines common interactions. The detailed implementation checklist and technical decisions govern functionality and defaults. Fixed sample values are illustrative.

## Implementation rules

- Source keys identify reusable screens, forms, variants or examples, not necessarily routes.
- Mobile keys use `mobile` followed by the capitalized source key. All explicit counterparts are linked in the inventory.
- Without a dedicated counterpart, use the same fields/actions inside the defined full-screen mobile editor or inspector. Do not remove functionality.
- Filled, Validation, Saving and Saved variants belong to the matching editor. Use the shared save-failure/conflict states for all forms.
- Record-specific examples use shared components with live data.
- A prototype `P / key` maps to the source key; use its source reference for visual implementation.
- Infrastructure requirements have behavior tests and documentation rather than invented Figma screens.

## Responsive rules

| Width | Navigation | Task detail |
|---|---|---|
| ≥1280 | 200px sidebar | 352px docked |
| 1024–1279 | 72px rail | Docked |
| 768–1023 | Rail | 352px overlay |
| ≤767 | Mobile navigation | Full-screen |

## Corrections and superseded examples

- Use current shared-workspace restore upload/preview/confirmation/maintenance designs instead of older restore screens (`20:2003`, `21:1529`, `26:1126`, `26:1151`, `22:437`, `27:376`).
- Use current `saveFailed` and draft recovery rather than old `21:2567` / `27:351`.
- Mobile plant ownership must target the plant task, not presentation ownership.
- Reference insurance image, reset checklist and mobile mode failure remain required despite disconnected prototype examples.
- Mobile links to desktop examples become responsive implementations with retained task/person/project context.
- `63:6176` is the mobile water-filter reference-application result; `64:40268` is the actual task-detail example. Keep both.
- Generic saving/saved examples are component references; specific editor variants take precedence.
- Prototype entry, atlases and archived explorations are review-only.
